local function decode(s)local r={}local i=1 while i<=#s do local p1=s:sub(i,i)local p2=s:sub(i+1,i+1)local p3=s:sub(i+2,i+2)local p4=s:sub(i+3,i+3)local c1=g[p1]local c2=g[p2]local c3=(p3=="=")and 0 or g[p3]local c4=(p4=="=")and 0 or g[p4]if c1==nil or c2==nil then return nil end if p3~="="and c3==nil then return nil end if p4~="="and c4==nil then return nil end r[#r+1]=string.char(c1*4+math.floor(c2/16))if p3~="="then r[#r+1]=string.char((c2%16)*16+math.floor(c3/4))end if p4~="="then r[#r+1]=string.char((c3%4)*64+c4)end i=i+4 end return table.concat(r)end
local original=decode(encoded)
local baseEnv=((getgenv and getgenv())or(_G and _G)or _ENV)
local VM={}setmetatable(VM,{__index=function(_,k)return baseEnv[k]end,__newindex=function(_,k,v)rawset(VM,k,v)end})
local loader=loadstring or load local fn,err if setfenv then fn,err=loader(original)if not fn then return nil end setfenv(fn,VM)else fn,err=loader(original,nil,"t",VM)if not fn then fn,err=loader(original)if not fn then return nil end end end local args={...}return fn(table.unpack(args))end)()
